RECORD OF SERVICES

Public Services of Overseas Territories (including some members of Her Majesty’s Overseas Civil Service, and other officers, serving outside the dependent territories who remain eligible for transfer), Colonial Office and Crown Agents for Oversea Governments and Administrations

The Governor or Governor-General is styled “His Excellency” while actually administering a Government, and an officer administering a Government in the absence of the Governor or Governor-General is similarly styled. All Judges are addressed out of Court as “The Honourable”.

In order to save space the above titles are omitted in these notes and it is possible to include, owing to considerations of space, only those officers who are permanent holders of administrative, judicial, professional and technical appointments and who have had ten years’ service, except in a few cases of holders of important posts. It has been necessary to curtail the length of the notes and to use numerous abbreviations which in most cases will be self-explanatory but which in the following instances may require explanation:

Bah. . . . Bahama Islands.
Barb. . . . Barbados.
Basuto. . . Basutoland.
B.B.S. . . . High Commission Territories (Basuto., Bech., Swaz.).
Bech. . . . Bechuanaland Protectorate.
Berm. . . . Bermuda.
B. Hond. . . British Honduras.
B. Sol. Is. Prot. British Solomon Islands Protectorate.
Brun. . . . Brunei.
C.A. . . . Crown Agents for Oversea Governments and Administrations.
C.A.C. . . . Central African Council.
C.A.D. . . . Colonial Audit Department.
C.O. . . . Colonial Office.
C.R.O. . . . Commonwealth Relations Office.
Cyp. . . . Cyprus.
(D.)D.M.S. . . (Deputy) Director of Medical Services.
(D.)D.P.W. . . (Deputy) Director of Public Works.
(D.)D.V.S. . . (Deputy) Director of Veterinary Services.
D.O. . . . Dominions Office or District Officer.
D.O.S. . . . Directorate of Overseas (formerly Colonial) (Geodetic and Topographic) Surveys.
D.T.C. . . . Department of Technical Co-operation.
D.W.W.I. . . Development and Welfare Organization in the West Indies and British Section of the Caribbean Commission.
E.A.A.F.R.O. . . East African Agriculture and Forestry Research Organization.
E.A.G.C. . . East African Governor’s Conference.
E.A.H.C. . . East Africa High Commission.
E.A.C.S.O. . . East African Common Services Organisation.
E.A.R. & H. . . East African Railways and Harbours.
E.A.T.(T.)R.(&R.)O. . . East African Tsetse (and Trypanosomiasis) Research (and Reclamation) Organization.
E.A.V.R.O. . . East African Veterinary Research Organization.
F.M.S. . . . Federated Malay States.
F.O. . . . Foreign Office.
Gam. . . . Gambia.
Gib. . . . Gibraltar.
G. & E. Is. Col. Gilbert and Ellice Islands Colony.
G.C. . . . Gold Coast.
H.C.T. . . . High Commission Territories (formerly South African High Commission).
H.K. . . . Hong Kong.
I.C.T.A. . . Imperial College of Tropical Agriculture.
I.M.S. . . . Indian Medical Service.
J’ca . . . Jamaica.
Ken. . . . Kenya.
K.U.R. & H. . . Kenya and Uganda Railways and Harbours.
L.S.H.T.M. . . London School of Hygiene and Tropical Medicine.
L.S.T.M. . . Liverpool School of Tropical Medicine.
Mal. . . . Malayan Union or Malaya.
Maur. . . . Mauritius.
M.C.S. . . . Malayan Civil Service.
M.E. . . . Middle East.
M.E.C. . . . Member of the Executive Council.
M.H.A. . . . Member of the House of Assembly.
M.H.R. . . Member of the House of Representatives.
M.L.A. . . Member of the Legislative Assembly.
M.L.C. . . Member of the Legislative Council.
M.O.H. . . Medical Officer of Health.
N.B.A.C. . . N. Borneo Armed Constabulary.
N: Heb. . . New Hebrides.
Nig. . . Nigeria, or Federation of.
Nyas. . . Nyasaland.
O.A.G. . . Officer Administering the Government.
O.A.D. . . Overseas Audit Department.
O.D.M. . . Ministry of Overseas Development.
Pal. . . Palestine.
Par. U.S. of S. . . Parliamentary Under Secretary of State.
P.U.S. of S. . . Permanent Under Secretary of State.
R.W.A.F.F. . . Royal West African Frontier Force.
S.A. . . South Africa.

M.C.S., 1931; asst. sec. to Br. res., Selangor, 1932; asst. advr., Besut, 1933; dist. offr., Raub, 1936; asst. fin. sec., S'pore, 1939; asst. comsnr. for civ. def., Mal., 1940; prin. asst. sec., 1946; dep. econ. sec., 1948; ch. sec., Aden, 1949; col. sec. (later chief sec.), S'pore, 1953; gov. and c.-in-c., 1957; U.K. Commissioner and Yang di-Pertuan Negara, 1959; gov. and c.-in-c., N. Borneo, 1960-63.

VANNIASINGHAM, N. K.—b. 1916; ed. Anglo-Chinese Sch., Penang, and Raffles Coll., S'pore; teacher, cl. I, S'pore, 1941; English master, Raffles Instn., 1949; lecturer, teacher train. coll., 1952; supernumy. offr., min. of educ., 1957; prin., teacher train. coll., 1957.

VAN STRATEN, O. J.—b. 1927; ed. Grey Coll., Bloemfontein, Univ. Coll., Orange Free State, and Univ. of Natal; geologist, Bech. Prot., 1951; chemist/petrologist, 1954; dep. dir., geol. survey, 1960.

VARLEY, E. R.—b. 1906; ed. Leeds Central High Sch., Leeds Univ. and Royal Sch. of Mines; asst., mineral resources dept., Imp. Inst., 1939; senr. asst., 1945; senr. scientific offr., 1946; prin. scientific offr., mineral resources div., col. (later o'seas) geol. surveys, 1948; D.T.C., 1961; O.D.M., 1964.

VAUGHAN, J. H. H.—b. 1920; ed. Highbury County Sch.; mil. serv., 1940–46, R.A.F.; cler. offr., C.O., 1937; higher cler./exec. offr., 1947; higher exec. offr., 1951; secon. D.W.W.I., 1952–58; senr. exec. offr., 1960; ch. exec. offr., 1963.

VAUGHN-COKEE, F. A.—b. 1913; ed. Queen's Coll., B. Guiana, Govt. Train. Coll. for Teachers, and Inst. of Educ., London; asst. master, govt. train. coll., B. Guiana, 1950; master, 1953; principal, 1957.

VELLIN, L. M. A.—b. 1904; ed. Royal Coll., Maur., and Univ. of Marseilles; M.O., Maur., 1949; med. sup. health dept., 1961.

VENKATASAMY, G., O.B.E. (1964).—b. 1911; ed. Royal Coll., Maur.; temp. clk., Maur., 1934; 2nd gr. clk., Maur., 1935; 1st gr., 1947; offr. i/c, finan. control, 1950; asst. sec., 1956; prin. asst. sec., 1959.

VENNER, N. E.—b. 1921; ed. L.S.E., London Univ.; cl. III clk., Grenada, 1942; cl. II, 1944; cl. I, 1949; secon., O. & M. survey, St. V., 1951; prin. clk., 1954; asst. sec. (fin. and econ.), Wind. Is., 1958; prin. sec. (trade, planning and devt.); fin. sec., St. L., 1963.

VERDCOURT, B.—b. 1925; ed. Luton Gram. Sch., and Reading Univ.; research offr. botanist, E.A.herbarium, E.A.A.F.R.O., 1948–64.

VERMA, T. K.—b. 1920; ed. Marist Bros. H. Sch., Fiji; survr. cadet, Fiji, 1942; survr., 1949; staff survr., 1954.

VERNON, J. W., C.M.G. (1964).—b. 1915; ed. Wallasey Gram. Sch., and Emmanuel Coll., Camb.; mil. serv., 1943–45, w/cdr.; apptd. after compet. exam. asst. prin., min. of food, 1939; sci. offr., M.A.P. VERRIER, W. L. I.—b. 1907; ed. Blundell's Sch. and Univs. of Wales and London; M.O., Fiji, 1938; S.M.O., 1955.

VERSEY, H. R.—b. 1928; ed. Roundhay Sch., Leeds, and Leeds Univ.; geol., J'ca, 1949; senr. geol., 1959; dep. dir. of geological survs., 1961. (J'ca Govt. service.)

VIADER, P. H. F.—b. 1909; ed. Univ. Coll., London; asst. master, Maur., 1929; master, 1942; educ. offr., 1946; prin. training coll., 1953; ch. educ. offr., 1960–64; retd., re-appid. supvr., Mauritius students unit, London.

VICKERS, T. D., C.M.G. (1956).—b. 1916; ed. Eton and King's Coll. Camb.; mil. serv., 1940–45; cadet, col. admin. serv., 1938; C.O., 1938; G.C., 1950; cl. II, 1952; col. sec., B. Hond. 1953; Maur., 1960.

VINSON, J. L. J., O.B.E. (1963).—b. 1906; ed. St. Joseph Coll., Coll. of Agric. Maur., and Museums Assoc., London; sci. asst. agric. dept., Maur., 1928; mission to Cey., 1939; curator, Maur. Inst., 1941; dir. 1954.

VOLAK, Z.—b. 1922; ed. (secondary in Poland) and Fitzwilliam House, Camb.; mil. serv., 1940–45 (Polish forces); agric. asst., Nyas., 1949; asst. livestock offr., 1951; livestock offr., 1952; regional livestock offr., 1962. (Malawi Govt. service.)

WADDELL, Sir Alexander, K.C.M.G. (1959), C.M.G. (1955), D.S.C. (1944).—b. 1913; ed. Fettes Coll. and Edin. Univ.; nav. serv., 1942–44, lieut.; mil. serv., 1944–47, lt. col.; W.O., 1944; cadet, B. WADDINGTON, E., M.B.E. (1962).—b. 1918; ed. High Storrs Gram. Sch., Sheffield, and Christ Church, Oxford; mil. serv., 1940; D.O. Basuto, 1941; secon., registr., co-op. socs., 1954–57; sec. to exec. coun., 1960.

WAGGITT, R. L., C.P.M. (1965).—b. 1920; mil. serv., 1938–49, major; asst. supt., police, Mal., 1949; dep. supt., Aden, 1957; supt., police, 1958; senr. supt., 1963.

WAGNER, M. S., M.B.E.—b. 1917; ed. Rhenania Coll., Neuhausen, Switzerland; mil. serv., 1938–46, major; cadet N. Rhod., 1946; dist. offr., 1949; senr. dist. offr. II, 1959; asst. sec., 1962; under sec., 1964. (Zambia Govt. service.)

WAIGHT, J. A., M.B.E. (1962).—b. 1907; ed. St. John's Coll., B. Hond. and Toronto Univ.; apprentice survr., B. Hond., 1924; govt. survr., 1928; senr. survr., 1931; dir., surveys, 1935.

WAINWRIGHT, R. E., C.M.G. (1959).—b. 1913; ed. Marlborough and Trinity Coll., Camb.; cadet, Ken., 1935; provincial comsnr., 1954; ch. comsnr., 1960–63; retd., apptd. acting admnr., Turks and Caicos Is., 1965.

WAINWRIGHT, R. G.—b. 1919; ed. King William's Coll. and Ramsey Gram. Sch.; mil. serv., 1940–46; Br. P.O., 1936–47; techn. gr. I, E.A. posts and tels dept., 1947; inspgr. techn., E.A.P. & T. admin., 1953; asst. engrn., gr. I, 1955; senr. asst. engrn., 1956; senr. sect. engrn., 1962.

WAKEFIELD, J. T.—b. 1915; ed. Manchester Gram. Sch. and Coll. of Tech.; mil. serv., 1939–46, capt. R.E.; cadet offr., cl. II, H.K., 1947; mem., bd. of examrs. in Chinese, 1949; cl. I offr., 1957; admin. offr., staff gr. B, 1959; asst. col. sec. (lands), 1960.

WALDRON, C. A.—b. 1923; ed. Christian Bros. Coll., S.A.; mil. serv., 1943–45; agric. offr., Tang., 1953. (Tanzania Govt. service.)

WALFORD, A. S.—b. 1905; ed. Charterhouse and Oriel Coll., Oxford; educ. offr., Ken., 1928; senr., 1948; registr., co-op. socs., 1953. (Ken. Govt. service.)

WALFORD, D. S., B.E.M., C.P.M. (1964).—b. 1923; ed. Aldeburgh Lodge Sch., and Bradfield Coll.; mil. serv., 1942–46/48–50; asst. supt., Mal. police, 1951; dep. supt., B.S.I.P., 1957; secon. Br. commdt., police, N. Heb., 1959.

WALKER, C.—b. 1928; ed. Marist Bros. High Sch., Fiji and Auck. Univ., N.Z.; agric. asst., Fiji 1953; senr. agric. asst., 1954; agric. offr., 1957; senr. agric. offr., 1963.

WALKER, C. A.—b. 1914; ed. Minister & Mundella Sec. Sch., Notts., Notts. Univ.; I.C.T.A., animal husbandry offr., N. Rhod. (Zambia Govt. service.)

WALKER, D. A. W.—b. 1921; ed. Prince of Wales Sch., Nairobi, Caterham Sch., and R. Vet. WALKER, D. W.—b. 1917; ed. Highgate Sch. and London Univ.; mil. serv., 1939-46, lieut. R.N.; mech. and elec. engrnr., Uga., 1948; chief mech. engrnr., 1954; chief electr. and mech. engrnr., H.K., 1958; asst. D.P.W., 1961; redesign. govt. elec. and mech. engrnr., 1964.

WALKER, E.—b. 1925; ed. Barnard Castle Sch., and Durham Univ.; mil. serv., 1944-49; survr., Fiji, 1951; staff survr., 1954.

WALKER, G. W.—b. 1925; ed. Municipal Tech. Coll., Hull; chemist, Tang., 1950; govt. chemist, 1961-64. (Tanzania Govt. service.)

WALKER, H. S.—b. 1924; ed. Kingston Coll., J'ca, McGill and Manchester Univs.; barrister-at-law, Gray's Inn; admin. asst. to devel. comsnr., B. Guiana, 1950; asst. sec., devel. sec't., 1953; prin. asst. sec., 1955; perm. sec., 1957; asst. under sec., J'ca, 1957. (J'ca Govt. service.)

WALKER, J.—b. 1908; ed. Morgan Acad., Dundee; mil. serv., 1940-45; acctnt., Uga., 1950; chief acctnt. and stat. offr., E.A. customs and excise, 1952.

WALKER, Miss J. B.—ed. Retford High Sch. and L'pool Univ.; research offr. (entom.) E.A.V.R.O., 1949; prin. scient. offr., 1961.

WALKER, O. A.—b. 1912; ed. Rawle Inst. and Inst. of Educ. London Univ.; headmnr., primary sch., St. L., 1938; asst. inspr., schs., 1948; inspr., 1949; educ. offr., Dominica, 1955.

WALKER, R. R.—b. 1912; ed. St. Joseph's Acad., Blackheath; mil. serv., 1939-45, maj., (desps.); dist. asst., N. Rhod., 1946; supvr., co-op. soc., 1948; senr. co-op. and marktg. offr., 1956; prin. co-op. and marktg. offr., 1962. (Zambia Govt. service.)

WALLACE, W. W., O.B.E. (1964), D.S.C. (1944).—b. 1923; ed. George Heriot's Sch.; mil. serv., 1942-46, capt., R.M.; cadet, S. Leone, 1948; dist. comsnr., 1954; secon., C.O., 1955-57; senr. dist. comsrr., 1961; prov. comsnr., 1961; dev. sec., 1962-64; retd., apptd estab. sec., Bah., 1964.

WALLER, J. W.—b. 1916; ed. Edmonton and Purley County Secondary Schs.; mil. serv., 1940-46, lieut. R.N.; Br. P.O., 1936-50; acctnt., E.A. posts and tels. dept., 1950; senr. acctnt., 1953; prin. acctnt., 1960; contr. sav. bk., 1961.

WALLINGTON, H. G.—b. 1921; ed. Wellingborough Sch., and Downing Coll., Camb.; mil. serv., 1940-46, maj.; admin. offr., B.S.I.P., 1950; secon. admin. offr., N. Hebs., 1958.

WALLIS, P. S.—b. 1914; inspr. (water), P.W.D., N. Nig., 1946; E. Nig., 1947; inspr., gr. II, 1951; ch. inspr. (water), 1958; wks. manager, 1960; ch. water supply supt., 1961. (Nig. Govt. service.)

WALLWORK, G.—b. 1921; ed. Haberdashers' Aske's Sch., Hampstead, City and Guilds Coll.; mil. serv., 1942-46, lt.; asst. engrnr., T.R. and P.S., 1946; dist. engrnr., E.A.R. & H., 1953; prin. asst. (research and plan.), 1956.

WALNE, J. W. W., C.P.M. (1961).—b. 1922; ed. Sydney H. Sch., Aust.; mil. serv., 1941-46, A.I.F., capt.; asst. supt. police, N. Borneo, 1946; Mal., 1948-49; asst. supt., N. Borneo, 1951; dep. supt., 1958-64.

WALSH, E. P.—b. 1928; ed. The Oratory Sch.; mil. serv., 1945-49; agric. supvr., N. Rhod., 1954; lab. offr., 1955; senr., 1962; senr. prin., 1963; asst. sec., 1964. (Zambia Govt. service.)
